In serine hydroxymethyltransferase, the catalytic residues typically include:
- A histidine that acts as an electrophile (or helps in the reaction)
- A glutamate that acts as a proton acceptor

- In SHMT, His97 (or equivalent) is known to act as an electrophile
- Glu145 (or equivalent) is known to act as a proton acceptor

- His97 acts as the electrophile (it helps form the Schiff base intermediate or participates in the formaldehyde transfer)
- Glu145 acts as the proton acceptor (it abstracts a proton during the reaction)
